=$0000						ram_start = $0000           ; start of installed RAM, must include zpage
=$0000						zpage     = ram_start       ; begin of Zero Page usage ($0000-$00ff)
=$0100						stack0    = $0100           ; begin of Return Stack ($0100-$01ff)
=$7f						zpage_end = $7F             ; last byte (inclusive) of Zero Page reserved for Tali ($0000-$007f)
=32767						ram_end   = $8000-1         ; end of installed RAM
=31744						hist_buff = ram_end-$03ff   ; begin of history buffers
=$ff						rsp0      = $ff             ; initial Return Stack Pointer (65c02 stack)
=$ff						bsize     = $ff             ; size of input/output buffers
=$0000						user0     = zpage           ; start of TaliForth2 system variables
=512						buffer0   = stack0+$100     ; input buffer ($0200-$02ff)
=768						cp0       = buffer0+bsize+1 ; Dictionary starts after last buffer
=31744						cp_end    = hist_buff       ; Last RAM byte available for code
=$ff						padoffset = $ff             ; offset from CP to PAD (holds number strings)
=120						dsp0      = zpage_end-7     ; initial Data Stack Pointer, used to initialize the X register.
.b31d						cold_zp_table:
>b31d	0000	00 08				cp:         .word cp0+256+1024      ; Compiler Pointer
>b31f	0002	51 c0				dp:         .word dictionary_start  ; Dictionary Pointer
>b321	0004	00 00				ip:         .word 0                 ; Instruction Pointer (current xt)
>b323	0006	00 00				workword:   .word 0                 ; nt (not xt!) of word being compiled, except in
>b325	0008	00 03				up:         .word cp0               ; Forth user vars at start of available RAM
>b327	000a	00 00				insrc:      .word 0                 ; input source for SOURCE-ID (0 for keyboard)
>b329	000c	00 02				cib:        .word buffer0           ; address of current input buffer
>b32b	000e	00 00				ciblen:     .word 0                 ; length of current input buffer
>b32d	0010	00 00				toin:       .word 0                 ; pointer to CIB (>IN in Forth)
>b32f	0012	22 f0				output:     .word kernel_putc       ; vector for EMIT
